- **app-motion**: Phase 8 of app-craft. Adds motion that is feedback and continuity — never spectacle. Hover/focus ≤120ms, nothing over 300ms, transform/opacity only, optimistic UI, shared-element continuity, direction-bearing enter/exit, pending states that don't reflow. Explicitly bans landing-page motion (scroll-jacking, smooth-scroll libraries, count-ups, parallax) from application UI. Reads the built app; applies app-craft/references/app-motion.md. ([propiter/landing-craft](https://github.com/propiter/landing-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/app-motion.md) · ★1)
- **app-movil**: Implementa pantallas y logica de la app Expo consumiendo la API. Usar cuando el contrato de la API ya este en la especificacion; no toca la API. ([Tuarez2/barberly](https://github.com/Tuarez2/barberly/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-movil.md))
- **app-onboarding**: Guide activation with server-derived progress and accessible Svelte checklists. ([legioncodeinc/develop-like-a-girl-advanced-resources](https://github.com/legioncodeinc/develop-like-a-girl-advanced-resources/blob/HEAD/plugins/dlag-advanced-app-builder/agents/app-onboarding.md))
- **app-onboarding**: Add a simple accessible Starter onboarding checklist tied to verified application state. ([legioncodeinc/develop-like-a-girl-starter-resources](https://github.com/legioncodeinc/develop-like-a-girl-starter-resources/blob/HEAD/plugins/dlag-starter-app-builder/agents/app-onboarding.md))
- **app-orchestrator**: Webアプリの準備→要件定義→設計→実装→公開→品質ゲートを実行する内部オーケストレーター。Claude Codeの /build-app /improve-app、Codexの $build-app / $improve-app から明示的に委譲された場合、custom agent起動時、または利用者が $app-orchestrator を明示した場合だけ使用する。一般のアプリ相談から暗黙起動しない。 ([daishiman/harness-dev](https://github.com/daishiman/harness-dev/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-orchestrator.md) · ★10 · 2 copies)
- **app-orchestrator**: Chef d'orchestre de FBMS. Coordonne les agents, distribue les tâches, consolide les résultats et empêche deux agents d'écrire dans le même fichier. ([nwodobe/fbms](https://github.com/nwodobe/fbms/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-orchestrator.md))
- **app-owner**: Owns the composition root and the store — src/app/, src/store/, src/main.tsx. Use for the profile switch, RTK Query registration, slices, typed hooks, or wiring a feature into the app. Never edits src/shared/ or the inside of a feature. ([marinvch/weather-app](https://github.com/marinvch/weather-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-owner.md))
- **app-performance-engineer**: Use when you need to profile and improve application performance safely — frontend budgets and Core Web Vitals, backend latency and flame graphs, caching, and memory or resource leaks. This agent measures first, then makes focused, verified changes. ([FluxonLab/Skillry](https://github.com/FluxonLab/Skillry/blob/HEAD/plugins/performance-and-cost/agents/app-performance-engineer.md) · ★10)
- **app-persona**: Plays ONE named persona of this product, using the real app in character, and reports friction as that person would feel it during a working day. Use one agent per persona so their verdicts stay independent. Judges trust, copy and fit to the job, not only whether buttons work. Reads the personas from the product's domain skill and does nothing until they are written. Does not edit files. ([ajaynarang/app-foundation](https://github.com/ajaynarang/app-foundation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-persona.md))
- **app-planner**: Guide users from app idea to feature breakdown to implementation plan. Use proactively when the user has a new app idea, wants to start a project from scratch, or needs help breaking down an app concept into features and specs. Helps bridge the gap between vision and systematic implementation. ([larus-peritus/pel_web](https://github.com/larus-peritus/pel_web/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-planner.md))
- **app-platform-agent**: Orchestrates DigitalOcean App Platform deployments - web apps, APIs, workers, static sites ([vanman2024/ai-dev-marketplace](https://github.com/vanman2024/ai-dev-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/digitalocean/agents/app-platform-agent.md) · ★10)
- **app-platform-engineer**: Use this agent to choose and configure where an app runs on Azure — the compute decision (App Service / Container Apps / Functions Flex Consumption / Static Web Apps / AKS), scaling, deployment slots, and provisioning the Azure host for a Claude app or web front-end. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/azure-cloud/agents/app-platform-engineer.md) · ★9)
- **app-polish**: Phase 9 of app-craft — the craft pass. Tightens density, alignment, typography and spacing; builds the keyboard model (roving tabindex, shortcuts, focus management, ⌘K); verifies ARIA patterns; measures WCAG AA contrast in BOTH themes; and fixes responsive behaviour down to 320px and 200% zoom. Reads the built app; applies Impeccable + interaction-a11y + the contrast gate. ([propiter/landing-craft](https://github.com/propiter/landing-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/app-polish.md) · ★1)
- **app-privacy-compliance**: Reviews the privacy regulation and store-policy obligations that gate shipping, as engineering facts, not legal conclusions. Covers consent ordering, data minimization, deletion propagation, third-party SDKs as processors, and Privacy Manifests / ATT / Data Safety forms. Distinct from `security`, `web-analytics`, `platform-release`. ([austintheriot/dotfiles](https://github.com/austintheriot/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-privacy-compliance.md) · ★1)
- **app-product**: Phase 1 of app-craft — establishes WHAT the app does, WHO uses it (roles), the jobs-to-be-done ranked by frequency, and a teardown of the reference-class UIs in this category. The interface-focused analogue of market research. Reads app/_init.md; writes app/product.md. Leads autonomously — researches instead of interrogating. ([propiter/landing-craft](https://github.com/propiter/landing-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/app-product.md) · ★1)
- **app-prompt-engineer**: Compiles a settled app brief into a build-ready spec, or audits a spec for gaps. Dispatch after the interview — the AskUserQuestion interview stays in the main thread. ([OliverDolle/Dolle](https://github.com/OliverDolle/Dolle/blob/HEAD/plugins/devkit/agents/app-prompt-engineer.md))
- **app-prototyper**: Patterson internal-tools prototyper. Use for dashboard/app-shell prototypes: adapts the Skill Studio file-manager template (top bar, sidebar, grid) to new tool concepts on brand tokens. Works from the files bundled in the patterson-file-manager plugin. ([danielbodnar/patterson-design-plugins](https://github.com/danielbodnar/patterson-design-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/patterson-file-manager/agents/app-prototyper.md) · ★2)
- **app-qa**: Story-driven QA of the FUTO Notes clients — desktop Tauri, native iOS, native Android — including cross-client sync. Use when asked to QA a merge request, a feature, or a spec surface on any client — "QA this MR", "test this on Android/desktop", "run the editor stories on iOS", "do a QA pass". Safe to run in parallel with other sessions on the same machine — it claims isolated pooled devices per worktree and never touches devices it didn't claim. ([futo-org/futo-notes](https://github.com/futo-org/futo-notes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-qa.md) · ★54)
- **app-qa-runner**: Drives the running app end to end (the API with curl, the web app in a browser, the Flutter app on a simulator when there is one) and reports what it OBSERVED. Use when verifying a change against the real app, or for a scripted walk, so the long browser and HTTP transcript stays out of the caller's context. Reports evidence, never assumptions. Does not edit files. ([ajaynarang/app-foundation](https://github.com/ajaynarang/app-foundation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-qa-runner.md))
- **app-qa-tester**: Independent QA tester for a vibe-coding-to-sdd task or handover. Started by the vibe-coding-to-sdd skill at a task's validation step, with the QA-tester role brief and the task's input paths as its prompt. Starts the app with its recorded commands, runs the end-to-end cases, performs each acceptance criterion, and returns a results-and-findings report; reads no source code and writes only evidence. Do not use without that brief. ([devmitra/devmitra-ai-plugin](https://github.com/devmitra/devmitra-ai-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/app-qa-tester.md) · ★1)
- **app-registry-manager**: Manages the app registry in .msd/memory/apps.json — reads and writes app state, checks first-release status, discovers apps in directories ([ofcskn/mobile-automation-plugin](https://github.com/ofcskn/mobile-automation-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/app-registry-manager.md) · ★2)
- **app-release-engineer**: Get mobile builds to users predictably, with staged rollout and a kill switch that works. Invoke for mobile-team work. ([bakhod1r/awesome-agents](https://github.com/bakhod1r/awesome-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-release-engineer.md) · 2 copies)
- **app-release-manager**: Use this agent when preparing to release a new version of MirrorBuddy. Ensures educational content quality, student safety, GDPR compliance, accessibility standards (WCAG 2.1 AA), ISE Engineering Fundamentals compliance, and AI tutor readiness before any public release. ([FightTheStroke/MirrorBuddy](https://github.com/FightTheStroke/MirrorBuddy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-release-manager.md) · ★8)
- **app-remediate**: The rescue surgeon of app-craft — executes the audit's plan in verified waves on a branch. Wave 0 builds the safety net (strict TS, lint, build, route smoke tests, Playwright visual baselines), then: delete dead code → tokenize every hardcoded value → de-duplicate into real primitives → fix architecture → fix bugs and add every missing state → a11y and craft → performance. Verifies tsc/lint/build/smoke/visual-diff between every wave and commits each one separately. Never deletes without proof; never leaves a half-migration. ([propiter/landing-craft](https://github.com/propiter/landing-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/app-remediate.md) · ★1)
- **app-repo-scout**: Use when the target app or repo is ambiguous before explore or codegen. Read-only: graph search + runbook repo table → repo path, ship/verify commands, scoped explore prompt. Narrower than generic explore. ([mrkinglollipop/manna-links](https://github.com/mrkinglollipop/manna-links/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/app-repo-scout.md))
- **app-research**: Web research from current primary sources: business facts, prices, regulations, packages and APIs. ([Take-Group/tg-boiler-small](https://github.com/Take-Group/tg-boiler-small/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-research.md))
- **app-review**: Phase 10 (the gate) of app-craft. Renders every screen at 320/768/1440 in light and dark, drives every state via the mock scenarios, and scores the SIX bars plus the a11y gate, the states gate, the contract gate, the motion gate and the hardening gate. Returns PASS/FAIL with a structured findings list, each tagged with the phase that owns the fix. The orchestrator loops fixes → re-review, max 3 passes. Never returns a fake PASS. ([propiter/landing-craft](https://github.com/propiter/landing-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/app-review.md) · ★1)
- **app-review-auditor**: Read-only App Store rejection-risk audit of an Apple project's code, in one of two modes. mode=guidelines: App Review Guidelines sections 1-5 checked against code, including the 5.1.x App Privacy evidence table and the privacy-policy.md consistency check. mode=claims: every sentence in the store description / promo text gets a code anchor, with separate-target and FLOW-STUB checks (the source of 2.3.1 rejections). Every verdict carries file:line evidence. Dispatched by apple-dev:asc-submit-preview (mode=guidelines) and apple-dev:asc-listing (Step 3.5 → mode=claims; Mode B → mode=guidelines, p… ([n0rvyn/indie-toolkit](https://github.com/n0rvyn/indie-toolkit/blob/HEAD/apple-dev/agents/app-review-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **app-review-director**: Готовит сценарии и снимает видео для Apple App Review на подключённых физическом iPhone и iPad по точному TestFlight-кандидату: регистрация одноразового аккаунта, вход reviewer, жалоба/блокировка, удаление аккаунта, гостевые и основные сцены. Для «сними видео для Apple», «подготовь сцены App Review». Монтаж, Reply/Notes и ASC не трогает. ([kelios/metravel2](https://github.com/kelios/metravel2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-review-director.md) · 2 copies)
- **app-review-guardian**: App Store Review Guidelines expert. Catches rejection risks before submission: privacy manifests, IAP rules, HIG violations, entitlement issues, metadata problems, and common guideline misinterpretations. ([Techopolis/swift-agents](https://github.com/Techopolis/swift-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/app-review-guardian.md) · ★39 · 8 copies)
- **app-review-packager**: Собирает и проверяет пакет для Apple App Review по точному iOS-кандидату: покрытие сцен демо-видео, scene manifest, готовность вложения/ссылки, согласованность Reply/Notes с доказательствами. Для «хватит ли материалов Apple», «что не снято», «проверь пакет перед submit». Не записывает, не монтирует, ASC не трогает. ([kelios/metravel2](https://github.com/kelios/metravel2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-review-packager.md) · 2 copies)
- **app-review-simulator**: Use this agent to simulate Apple's App Review BEFORE you upload — it scans an iOS / iPadOS / macOS / visionOS project for the patterns that get apps rejected (Guideline violations, missing Sign in with Apple, no in-app account deletion, absent PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy, IAP-bypass payments, weak usage strings, missing ATT, unsafe UGC, iPad layout breakage, placeholder/debug content, screenshot and metadata problems) and returns a severity-ranked risk report with the exact guideline number, file:line, and a concrete fix. Use PROACTIVELY before any App Store / TestFlight-to-Store submission, after… ([Emasoft/emasoft-complete-ios-app-authoring](https://github.com/Emasoft/emasoft-complete-ios-app-authoring/blob/HEAD/agents/app-review-simulator.md) · ★3)
- **app-reviewer**: アプリケーション全体を包括的にレビューし、コード品質、アーキテクチャ、セキュリティ、パフォーマンスの改善提案を行います。 ([ohzono/native-app-fullcycle](https://github.com/ohzono/native-app-fullcycle/blob/HEAD/agents/app-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **app-reviewer**: Reviews a diff along ONE named dimension (correctness, authz-tenancy, contract, conventions, tests, performance, ui, mobile or domain) and returns structured findings. Use when fanning out a review across dimensions, as app-review does, so each verdict is reached independently. Read-only - it reports, it does not fix. ([ajaynarang/app-foundation](https://github.com/ajaynarang/app-foundation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-reviewer.md))
- **app-reviewer**: Alias for app-reviewer-coordinator. Orchestrates the Builder APP_REVIEW stage by fanning out to three read-only sub-reviewers (security, correctness, build) in parallel, then merging their findings into a single review report. Prefer invoking `app-reviewer-coordinator` directly in new code. ([LukaLeskovsek/makers-and-breakers-claude-marketplace](https://github.com/LukaLeskovsek/makers-and-breakers-claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/harness/agents/app-reviewer.md))
- **app-reviewer-build**: Read-only build/tooling review for Builder apps. Checks the changed files for invalid Tailwind classes, wrong App Router conventions, dependency resolution issues, and build-adjacent problems. Spawned as a sub-agent by app-reviewer-coordinator. ([LukaLeskovsek/makers-and-breakers-claude-marketplace](https://github.com/LukaLeskovsek/makers-and-breakers-claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/harness/agents/app-reviewer-build.md))
- **app-reviewer-coordinator**: Coordinates the Builder APP_REVIEW stage by fanning out to three read-only sub-reviewers (security, correctness, build) in parallel, then merging their findings into a single review report. Preserves the P0/P1/P2 shape that downstream tasks expect. ([LukaLeskovsek/makers-and-breakers-claude-marketplace](https://github.com/LukaLeskovsek/makers-and-breakers-claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/harness/agents/app-reviewer-coordinator.md))
- **app-reviewer-correctness**: Read-only correctness review for Builder apps. Checks the changed files for broken imports, missing deps, type errors, race conditions, and missing error handling. Spawned as a sub-agent by app-reviewer-coordinator. ([LukaLeskovsek/makers-and-breakers-claude-marketplace](https://github.com/LukaLeskovsek/makers-and-breakers-claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/harness/agents/app-reviewer-correctness.md))
